    /home/vumenira/projects/Air_raid_alert_analysis/app/static/app.js
    /* Air Raid Forecast dashboard: map + ranking + region detail + test-period statistics. */
    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const css = (v) => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(v).trim();
    
    const state = { meta: null, mode: "replay", idx: 0, snap: null, selected: null, timer: null, nowTimer: null, geo: null };
    
    const fmtKyiv = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", { timeZone: "Europe/Kyiv", weekday: "short", day: "2-digit", month: "short", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    const fmtShort = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", { timeZone: "Europe/Kyiv", day: "2-digit", month: "short" });
    const fmtHM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", { timeZone: "Europe/Kyiv", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    const pct = (p) => `${Math.round(p * 100)}%`;
    const mins = (m) => (m >= 90 ? `${Math.floor(m / 60)}h ${String(Math.round(m % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}m` : `${Math.round(m)} min`);
    const label = (r) => state.meta.regions.find((x) => x.region === r)?.label ?? r;
    
    // Discrete sequential scale (one hue, dark -> light on a dark surface)
    const RAMP = ["--seq-0", "--seq-1", "--seq-2", "--seq-3", "--seq-4", "--seq-5"].map(css);
    const riskColor = d3.scaleThreshold().domain([0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65]).range(RAMP);
    
    /* ---------- tooltip ---------- */
    const tip = $("tooltip");
    function showTip(ev, html) {
      tip.innerHTML = html; tip.hidden = false;
      const { innerWidth: W, innerHeight: H } = window;
      const r = tip.getBoundingClientRect();
      tip.style.left = `${Math.min(ev.clientX + 14, W - r.width - 8)}px`;
      tip.style.top = `${Math.min(ev.clientY + 14, H - r.height - 8)}px`;
    }
    const hideTip = () => (tip.hidden = true);
    
    /* ---------- data ---------- */
    const getJSON = async (url) => {
      const r = await fetch(url);
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${url}: ${r.status} ${await r.text()}`);
      return r.json();
    };
    
    async function loadSnapshot() {
      try {
        state.snap = state.mode === "replay"
          ? await getJSON(`/api/replay?t=${encodeURIComponent(state.meta.times[state.idx])}`)
          : await getJSON("/api/now");
      } catch (e) {
        $("status-line").innerHTML = `<span class="warn">${e.message}</span>`;
        return;
      }
      render();
    }
    
    /* ---------- map ---------- */
    let mapSel = null;
    function drawMap() {
      const el = $("map");
      el.innerHTML = "";
      const { width: w, height: h } = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      // Planar projection (robust to polygon winding): x scaled by cos(latitude) to keep proportions.
      const c = Math.cos((48.5 * Math.PI) / 180);
      const flat = d3.geoTransform({ point(x, y) { this.stream.point(x * c, -y); } });
      const [[x0, y0], [x1, y1]] = d3.geoPath(flat).bounds(state.geo);
      const k = Math.min((w - 20) / (x1 - x0), (h - 20) / (y1 - y0));
      const ox = (w - k * (x1 - x0)) / 2, oy = (h - k * (y1 - y0)) / 2;
      const proj = d3.geoTransform({ point(x, y) { this.stream.point(ox + k * (x * c - x0), oy + k * (-y - y0)); } });
      const path = d3.geoPath(proj);
    
      // draw Kyiv City last so it stays clickable on top of Kyiv oblast
      const feats = [...state.geo.features].sort((a, b) => (a.properties.region === "Kyiv City") - (b.properties.region === "Kyiv City"));
      const svg = d3.select(el).append("svg").attr("viewBox", `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
      const shapes = svg.append("g").selectAll("path").data(feats).join("path")
        .attr("class", (d) => `oblast${d.properties.permanent ? " perm" : ""}`)
        .attr("d", path)
        .on("mousemove", (ev, d) => mapTip(ev, d))
        .on("mouseleave", hideTip)
        .on("click", (ev, d) => !d.properties.permanent && select(d.properties.region));
      const truth = svg.append("g"), active = svg.append("g"), selected = svg.append("g");
      const labels = svg.append("g").selectAll("g")
        .data(feats.filter((d) => !d.properties.permanent && d.properties.region !== "Kyiv City"))
        .join("g").attr("transform", (d) => `translate(${path.centroid(d)})`);
      labels.append("text").attr("class", "map-label").attr("dy", -2).text((d) => label(d.properties.region));
      labels.append("text").attr("class", "map-label val").attr("dy", 10);
      mapSel = { shapes, truth, active, selected, labels, path, feats };
    }
    
    function mapTip(ev, d) {
      const r = d.properties.region;
      if (d.properties.permanent) return showTip(ev, `<b>${r}</b><br><span class="mono">Permanent alert since 2022 - not modelled</span>`);
      const s = state.snap?.regions.find((x) => x.region === r);
      if (!s) return;
      let html = `<b>${label(r)}</b> ${s.active ? '<span class="tag alert">ALERT</span>' : ""}<br>P(start ≤ 1 h): <b>${pct(s.p_start)}</b>`;
      if (s.p_baseline !== undefined) html += `<br><span class="mono">baseline ${pct(s.p_baseline)} · actual: ${s.actual_start_next_hour ? "started" : "no start"}</span>`;
      if (s.duration) html += `<br><span class="mono">expected end in ~${mins(Math.max(s.duration.median_min - s.duration.elapsed_min, 0))}</span>`;
      showTip(ev, html);
    }
    
    function renderMap() {
      const byRegion = Object.fromEntries(state.snap.regions.map((r) => [r.region, r]));
      const { shapes, truth, active, selected, labels, path, feats } = mapSel;
      shapes.filter((d) => !d.properties.permanent).attr("fill", (d) => riskColor(byRegion[d.properties.region]?.p_start ?? 0));
      const outline = (g, cls, pred) => g.selectAll("path").data(feats.filter(pred), (d) => d.properties.region)
        .join("path").attr("class", cls).attr("d", path);
      outline(truth, "outline-truth", (d) => byRegion[d.properties.region]?.actual_start_next_hour);
      outline(active, "outline-active", (d) => byRegion[d.properties.region]?.active);
      outline(selected, "outline-selected", (d) => d.properties.region === state.selected);
      labels.select(".val").text((d) => pct(byRegion[d.properties.region]?.p_start ?? 0));
    }
    
    /* ---------- ranking ---------- */
    function renderRanking() {
      const rows = [...state.snap.regions].sort((a, b) => b.p_start - a.p_start);
      d3.select("#ranking").selectAll("li").data(rows, (d) => d.region).join("li")
        .attr("class", (d) => (d.region === state.selected ? "selected" : null))
        .on("click", (ev, d) => select(d.region))
        .html((d, i) => `
          <span class="rank">${String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
          <span class="name"><span>${label(d.region)}${d.active ? '<span class="tag alert">ALERT</span>' : ""}${d.actual_start_next_hour ? '<span class="tag hit">STARTED</span>' : ""}</span>
            <span class="bar"><div style="width:${d.p_start * 100}%;background:${riskColor(d.p_start)}"></div></span></span>
          <span class="val">${pct(d.p_start)}</span>`)
        .order();
    }
    
    /* ---------- region detail ---------- */
    async function renderDetail() {
      const r = state.selected;
      const s = state.snap.regions.find((x) => x.region === r);
      $("detail-title").innerHTML = `${label(r)} <span class="muted">${r}</span>`;
      const isReplay = state.mode === "replay";
      let html = `
        <div class="hero"><span class="num">${pct(s.p_start)}</span><span class="lbl">chance an alert starts<br>within the next hour</span></div>
        <div><span class="state ${s.active ? "alert" : "calm"}">${s.active ? "ALERT ACTIVE" : "NO ACTIVE ALERT"}</span></div>
        <dl class="kv">
          ${isReplay ? `<dt>Baseline (oblast × hour rate)</dt><dd>${pct(s.p_baseline)}</dd>
          <dt>Actual: alert started within the hour</dt><dd>${s.actual_start_next_hour ? "yes" : "no"}</dd>` : ""}
        </dl>`;
      if (s.duration) {
        const d = s.duration;
        html += `<div><div class="section-title">How long will it last?</div>
          <dl class="kv">
            ${d.started_at ? `<dt>Started</dt><dd>${fmtHM.format(new Date(d.started_at))} (${mins(d.elapsed_min)} ago)</dd>` : `<dt>Started</dt><dd>unknown (live feed)</dd>`}
            <dt>Most likely total (median)</dt><dd>${mins(d.median_min)}</dd>
            <dt>80% range</dt><dd>${mins(d.p10_min)} – ${mins(d.p90_min)}</dd>
            ${d.actual_min != null ? `<dt>Actual (revealed)</dt><dd>${mins(d.actual_min)}</dd>` : ""}
          </dl>
          <div id="survival" class="chart" style="height:150px"></div>
          ${!d.started_at ? '<div class="note">Live feed has no start time, so the distribution is not conditioned on elapsed time.</div>' : ""}</div>`;
      }
      html += `<div><div class="section-title">Last 7 days</div><div id="history" class="chart" style="height:46px"></div></div>`;
      if (isReplay) html += `<div><div class="section-title">Forecast vs actual · test period</div>
        <div class="chart-legend"><span><i style="background:var(--series-model)"></i>P(start ≤ 1 h)</span><span><i style="background:var(--text-primary);width:2px;height:8px"></i>alert started</span></div>
        <div id="series" class="chart" style="height:120px"></div></div>`;
      $("detail").innerHTML = html;
      if (s.duration) drawSurvival(s.duration);
    
      const t = state.snap.t;
      const hist = await getJSON(`/api/region?region=${encodeURIComponent(r)}&t=${encodeURIComponent(t)}&live=${!isReplay}`);
      if (state.selected !== r || state.snap.t !== t) return; // stale response
      drawHistory(hist.history, new Date(t));
      if (isReplay) drawSeries(hist.series, new Date(t));
    }
    
    function frame(el, m = { t: 8, r: 12, b: 20, l: 34 }) {
      const { width: w, height: h } = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const svg = d3.select(el).html("").append("svg").attr("viewBox", `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
      return { svg, w, h, m, iw: w - m.l - m.r, ih: h - m.t - m.b, g: svg.append("g").attr("transform", `translate(${m.l},${m.t})`) };
    }
    
    function drawSurvival(d) {
      const f = frame($("survival"));
      const grid = state.meta.curve_grid;
      const x = d3.scaleLinear([0, grid.at(-1)], [0, f.iw]);
      const y = d3.scaleLinear([0, 1], [f.ih, 0]);
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").attr("transform", `translate(0,${f.ih})`).call(d3.axisBottom(x).ticks(6).tickFormat((v) => `${v}m`));
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").call(d3.axisLeft(y).ticks(3).tickFormat(d3.format(".0%")));
      f.g.append("path").datum(grid.map((g, i) => [g, d.curve[i]])).attr("fill", "none")
        .attr("stroke", css("--series-model")).attr("stroke-width", 2).attr("d", d3.line((p) => x(p[0]), (p) => y(p[1])));
      const vline = (v, color, dash, text) => {
        if (v == null || v > grid.at(-1)) return;
        f.g.append("line").attr("x1", x(v)).attr("x2", x(v)).attr("y1", 0).attr("y2", f.ih).attr("stroke", color).attr("stroke-dasharray", dash);
        f.g.append("text").attr("class", "series-label").attr("x", x(v) + 3).attr("y", 9).text(text);
      };
      if (d.elapsed_min > 0) vline(d.elapsed_min, css("--text-muted"), "2 2", "now");
      vline(d.median_min, css("--series-model"), "4 3", "median");
      if (d.actual_min != null) vline(d.actual_min, css("--text-primary"), "1 2", "actual");
      // hover: crosshair + P(still active)
      const hover = f.g.append("line").attr("class", "cursor").attr("y1", 0).attr("y2", f.ih).style("display", "none");
      f.g.append("rect").attr("width", f.iw).attr("height", f.ih).attr("fill", "transparent")
        .on("mousemove", (ev) => {
          const m = Math.max(1, Math.min(grid.at(-1), Math.round(x.invert(d3.pointer(ev)[0]))));
          hover.style("display", null).attr("x1", x(m)).attr("x2", x(m));
          showTip(ev, `${m} min after start<br>P(still active): <b>${pct(d.curve[m - 1])}</b>`);
        })
        .on("mouseleave", () => { hover.style("display", "none"); hideTip(); });
    }
    
    function drawHistory(intervals, t) {
      const f = frame($("history"), { t: 4, r: 12, b: 18, l: 12 });
      const x = d3.scaleTime([new Date(t - 7 * 864e5), t], [0, f.iw]);
      f.g.append("rect").attr("width", f.iw).attr("height", f.ih).attr("fill", css("--panel-2")).attr("rx", 2);
      f.g.selectAll("rect.a").data(intervals).join("rect").attr("class", "a")
        .attr("x", (d) => x(new Date(d.start))).attr("width", (d) => Math.max(1.5, x(new Date(d.end)) - x(new Date(d.start))))
        .attr("height", f.ih).attr("fill", css("--critical"))
        .on("mousemove", (ev, d) => showTip(ev, `${fmtKyiv.format(new Date(d.start))} → ${fmtHM.format(new Date(d.end))}`))
        .on("mouseleave", hideTip);
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").attr("transform", `translate(0,${f.ih})`).call(d3.axisBottom(x).ticks(7).tickFormat((v) => fmtShort.format(v)));
      f.svg.append("text").attr("class", "series-label").attr("x", f.w - 12).attr("y", 12).attr("text-anchor", "end").text(`${intervals.length} alerts`);
    }
    
    function drawSeries(series, t) {
      const f = frame($("series"));
      const ts = series.t.map((v) => new Date(v));
      const x = d3.scaleTime(d3.extent(ts), [0, f.iw]);
      const y = d3.scaleLinear([0, 1], [f.ih, 0]);
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").attr("transform", `translate(0,${f.ih})`).call(d3.axisBottom(x).ticks(4).tickFormat((v) => fmtShort.format(v)));
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").call(d3.axisLeft(y).ticks(3).tickFormat(d3.format(".0%")));
      f.g.selectAll("line.hit").data(ts.filter((_, i) => series.y[i])).join("line").attr("class", "hit")
        .attr("x1", (d) => x(d)).attr("x2", (d) => x(d)).attr("y1", f.ih).attr("y2", f.ih - 6).attr("stroke", css("--text-primary")).attr("stroke-opacity", 0.6);
      f.g.append("path").datum(ts.map((d, i) => [d, series.p[i]])).attr("fill", "none").attr("stroke", css("--series-model"))
        .attr("stroke-width", 1.2).attr("d", d3.line((p) => x(p[0]), (p) => y(p[1])));
      f.g.append("line").attr("class", "cursor").attr("x1", x(t)).attr("x2", x(t)).attr("y1", 0).attr("y2", f.ih);
      hoverTime(f, x, ts, (i) => `${fmtKyiv.format(ts[i])}<br>P(start ≤ 1 h): <b>${pct(series.p[i])}</b><br><span class="mono">actual: ${series.y[i] ? "started" : "no start"}</span>`);
    }
    
    function hoverTime(f, x, ts, html, onClick) {
      const bis = d3.bisector((d) => d).center;
      const line = f.g.append("line").attr("class", "cursor").attr("stroke-opacity", 0.5).attr("y1", 0).attr("y2", f.ih).style("display", "none");
      f.g.append("rect").attr("width", f.iw).attr("height", f.ih).attr("fill", "transparent").style("cursor", onClick ? "pointer" : null)
        .on("mousemove", (ev) => {
          const i = bis(ts, x.invert(d3.pointer(ev)[0]));
          line.style("display", null).attr("x1", x(ts[i])).attr("x2", x(ts[i]));
          showTip(ev, html(i));
        })
        .on("mouseleave", () => { line.style("display", "none"); hideTip(); })
        .on("click", (ev) => onClick && onClick(bis(ts, x.invert(d3.pointer(ev)[0]))));
    }
    
    /* ---------- bottom statistics (static, test period) ---------- */
    function renderKpis() {
      const m = state.meta.metrics;
      const tile = (k, v, d, cls = "") => `<div class="kpi"><span class="k">${k}</span><span class="v">${v}</span><span class="d ${cls}">${d}</span></div>`;
      const rel = (a, b, lowerBetter) => {
        const diff = ((a - b) / b) * 100, better = lowerBetter ? diff < 0 : diff > 0;
        return [`${diff > 0 ? "+" : ""}${diff.toFixed(1)}% vs baseline ${b.toFixed(3)}`, better ? "better" : "worse"];
      };
      const [bd, bc] = rel(m.start_brier, m.start_baseline_brier, true);
      const [ad, ac] = rel(m.start_roc_auc, m.start_baseline_roc_auc, false);
      const [pd_, pc] = rel(m.start_pr_auc, m.start_baseline_pr_auc, false);
      const [md, mc] = rel(m.dur_mae_median_min, m.dur_mae_baseline_min, true);
      $("kpis").innerHTML =
        tile("Start · Brier", m.start_brier.toFixed(3), bd, bc) +
        tile("Start · ROC-AUC", m.start_roc_auc.toFixed(3), ad, ac) +
        tile("Start · PR-AUC", m.start_pr_auc.toFixed(3), pd_, pc) +
        tile("Duration · MAE", `${m.dur_mae_median_min.toFixed(1)}m`, md.replace(/baseline ([\d.]+)/, "baseline $1m"), mc) +
        tile("Duration · 80% cover", pct(m.dur_coverage_80), "target 80%", Math.abs(m.dur_coverage_80 - 0.8) < 0.05 ? "better" : "worse") +
        tile("Duration · C-index", m.dur_c_index.toFixed(3), `${m.dur_test_events} test alerts`);
    }
    
    function drawCalibration() {
      const f = frame($("calibration"), { t: 10, r: 14, b: 26, l: 36 });
      const x = d3.scaleLinear([0, 1], [0, f.iw]), y = d3.scaleLinear([0, 1], [f.ih, 0]);
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").attr("transform", `translate(0,${f.ih})`).call(d3.axisBottom(x).ticks(5).tickFormat(d3.format(".0%")));
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").call(d3.axisLeft(y).ticks(5).tickFormat(d3.format(".0%")));
      f.g.append("line").attr("x1", 0).attr("y1", f.ih).attr("x2", f.iw).attr("y2", 0).attr("class", "gridline").attr("stroke-dasharray", "3 3");
      f.g.append("text").attr("class", "series-label").attr("x", f.iw - 2).attr("y", 12).attr("text-anchor", "end").text("perfect");
      const data = state.meta.calibration;
      f.g.append("path").datum(data).attr("fill", "none").attr("stroke", css("--series-model")).attr("stroke-width", 2)
        .attr("d", d3.line((d) => x(d.mean_pred), (d) => y(d.frac_pos)));
      f.g.selectAll("circle").data(data).join("circle").attr("cx", (d) => x(d.mean_pred)).attr("cy", (d) => y(d.frac_pos)).attr("r", 4)
        .attr("fill", css("--series-model")).attr("stroke", css("--panel")).attr("stroke-width", 2)
        .on("mousemove", (ev, d) => showTip(ev, `predicted <b>${pct(d.mean_pred)}</b> → actual <b>${pct(d.frac_pos)}</b><br><span class="mono">${d.n} oblast-hours</span>`))
        .on("mouseleave", hideTip);
      f.svg.append("text").attr("class", "series-label").attr("x", f.m.l + f.iw / 2).attr("y", f.h - 1).attr("text-anchor", "middle").text("predicted probability");
    }
    
    let nationalCursor = null;
    function drawNational() {
      const f = frame($("national"), { t: 10, r: 14, b: 22, l: 30 });
      const n = state.meta.national, ts = n.t.map((v) => new Date(v));
      const x = d3.scaleTime(d3.extent(ts), [0, f.iw]), y = d3.scaleLinear([0, d3.max(n.active) || 1], [f.ih, 0]).nice();
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").attr("transform", `translate(0,${f.ih})`).call(d3.axisBottom(x).ticks(8).tickFormat((v) => fmtShort.format(v)));
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").call(d3.axisLeft(y).ticks(4));
      f.g.append("path").datum(ts.map((d, i) => [d, n.active[i]])).attr("fill", css("--series-model")).attr("fill-opacity", 0.18)
        .attr("d", d3.area((p) => x(p[0]), f.ih, (p) => y(p[1])));
      f.g.append("path").datum(ts.map((d, i) => [d, n.active[i]])).attr("fill", "none").attr("stroke", css("--series-model")).attr("stroke-width", 1.2)
        .attr("d", d3.line((p) => x(p[0]), (p) => y(p[1])));
      nationalCursor = f.g.append("line").attr("class", "cursor").attr("y1", 0).attr("y2", f.ih);
      nationalCursor.datum(x);
      hoverTime(f, x, ts, (i) => `${fmtKyiv.format(ts[i])}<br><b>${n.active[i]}</b> oblasts under alert<br><span class="mono">${n.starts[i]} alerts started in the next hour · model expected ${n.p_sum[i].toFixed(1)}</span><br><span class="mono">click to jump</span>`,
        (i) => { if (state.mode === "replay") setIdx(i); });
    }
    
    function drawImportance() {
      const names = { region: "Oblast", hour_kyiv: "Hour of day", dow_kyiv: "Day of week", active_now: "Alert active now", min_since_last_end: "Time since last alert",
        starts_24h: "Alerts last 24 h", starts_7d: "Alerts last 7 d", n_active_oblasts: "Oblasts on alert now", national_starts_1h: "Nationwide starts last 1 h" };
      const data = Object.entries(state.meta.importance).map(([k, v]) => ({ k: names[k] ?? k, v })).sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v);
      const total = d3.sum(data, (d) => d.v);
      const f = frame($("importance"), { t: 6, r: 40, b: 6, l: 150 });
      const y = d3.scaleBand(data.map((d) => d.k), [0, f.ih]).padding(0.3), x = d3.scaleLinear([0, d3.max(data, (d) => d.v)], [0, f.iw]);
      f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").call(d3.axisLeft(y).tickSize(0).tickPadding(8)).select(".domain").remove();
      f.g.selectAll("rect").data(data).join("rect").attr("y", (d) => y(d.k)).attr("height", y.bandwidth()).attr("width", (d) => x(d.v))
        .attr("rx", 2).attr("fill", css("--series-model"))
        .on("mousemove", (ev, d) => showTip(ev, `<b>${d.k}</b><br>${pct(d.v / total)} of total gain`)).on("mouseleave", hideTip);
      f.g.selectAll("text.v").data(data).join("text").attr("class", "series-label").attr("x", (d) => x(d.v) + 4)
        .attr("y", (d) => y(d.k) + y.bandwidth() / 2 + 3).text((d) => pct(d.v / total));
    }
    
    /* ---------- orchestration ---------- */
    function render() {
      const t = new Date(state.snap.t);
      $("clock-kyiv").textContent = `${fmtKyiv.format(t)} Kyiv`;
      $("clock-sub").textContent = state.mode === "replay"
        ? `forecast issued at ${t.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace("T", " ")} UTC`
        : `live · refreshes every 60 s`;
      $("status-line").innerHTML = state.mode === "replay"
        ? `test period ${fmtShort.format(new Date(state.meta.times[0]))} – ${fmtShort.format(new Date(state.meta.times.at(-1)))}`
        : `live state: ${state.snap.live_source ? "ubilling feed" : '<span class="warn">unavailable</span>'} · history until ${fmtKyiv.format(new Date(state.snap.history_until))}`;
      if (!state.selected) state.selected = [...state.snap.regions].sort((a, b) => b.p_start - a.p_start)[0].region;
      renderMap();
      renderRanking();
      renderDetail();
      if (nationalCursor) {
        const x = nationalCursor.datum();
        const inRange = state.mode === "replay";
        nationalCursor.style("display", inRange ? null : "none").attr("x1", x(t)).attr("x2", x(t));
      }
    }
    
    function select(region) {
      state.selected = region;
      renderMap(); renderRanking(); renderDetail();
    }
    
    function setIdx(i) {
      state.idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.meta.times.length - 1, i));
      $("slider").value = state.idx;
      loadSnapshot();
    }
    
    function setMode(mode) {
      state.mode = mode;
      document.body.classList.toggle("now", mode === "now");
      $("mode-replay").classList.toggle("active", mode === "replay");
      $("mode-now").classList.toggle("active", mode === "now");
      $("timeline").classList.toggle("disabled", mode === "now");
      stopPlay();
      clearInterval(state.nowTimer);
      if (mode === "now") state.nowTimer = setInterval(loadSnapshot, 60_000);
      loadSnapshot();
    }
    
    function stopPlay() { clearInterval(state.timer); state.timer = null; $("play").textContent = "▶"; }
    function togglePlay() {
      if (state.timer) return stopPlay();
      $("play").textContent = "❚❚";
      state.timer = setInterval(() => (state.idx >= state.meta.times.length - 1 ? stopPlay() : setIdx(state.idx + 1)), 700);
    }
    
    async function init() {
      [state.meta, state.geo] = await Promise.all([getJSON("/api/meta"), getJSON("/static/oblasts.geojson")]);
      $("ramp").style.background = `linear-gradient(90deg, ${RAMP.map((c, i) => `${c} ${(i / RAMP.length) * 100}% ${((i + 1) / RAMP.length) * 100}%`).join(",")})`;
      const slider = $("slider");
      slider.max = state.meta.times.length - 1;
      state.idx = state.meta.times.length - 1;
      slider.value = state.idx;
      let deb;
      slider.addEventListener("input", () => { stopPlay(); clearTimeout(deb); state.idx = +slider.value; deb = setTimeout(loadSnapshot, 80); });
      $("play").addEventListener("click", togglePlay);
      $("mode-replay").addEventListener("click", () => setMode("replay"));
      $("mode-now").addEventListener("click", () => setMode("now"));
      drawMap(); renderKpis(); drawCalibration(); drawNational(); drawImportance();
      window.addEventListener("resize", () => { drawMap(); drawCalibration(); drawNational(); drawImportance(); if (state.snap) render(); });
      await loadSnapshot();
    }
    
    init().catch((e) => { document.body.insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", `<pre class="warn" style="padding:16px">${e.message}</pre>`); });

    /home/vumenira/projects/Air_raid_alert_analysis/README.md
    # Air Raid Alert Forecast (Ukraine, oblast level)
    
    Two small models trained on the alert history, plus a dark "ops" dashboard to replay their forecasts:
    
    1. **Start model:** P(an air-raid alert starts in an oblast within the next hour). Uses LightGBM on hourly features.
    2. **Duration model:** the full distribution of how long an alert will last. Uses a log-normal survival model; the median is the point estimate, and the curve can be conditioned on time already elapsed.
    
    ## Quick start
    
    ```bash
    uv sync
    uv run python -m training.train        # ~20 s; downloads data, trains, writes models/ (add --no-wandb to skip W&B)
    uv run python -m app                   # open http://127.0.0.1:8000
    uv run pytest                          # leakage tests
    ```
    
    Trained models are committed in `models/`, so the dashboard also runs without retraining.
    
    ## Data
    
    - **Alerts:** [Vadimkin/ukrainian-air-raid-sirens-dataset](https://github.com/Vadimkin/ukrainian-air-raid-sirens-dataset), the volunteer (eTryvoga) file. It is oblast level, updated daily, all times in UTC. The official file in the same repo stops on 2026-09-07, so it can't be used to evaluate on the latest period.
    - **Live state ("Now" mode):** `ubilling.net.ua/aerialalerts`. It says which oblasts are on alert right now, but not when each alert started.
    - **Map:** geoBoundaries UKR ADM1 (ODbL).
    - **Excluded:** Luhansk oblast, Crimea and Sevastopol have been under permanent alerts since 2022, so they have no model.
    
    ## Evaluation (no leakage)
    
    - **Split:** time-ordered. Train from 2025-10-01; the test set is the last 28 days of data.
    - **Features at time t** use only alerts that started before t. Of those, only alerts already finished by t contribute their end time. `tests/test_no_leakage.py` recomputes features from data truncated at t and asserts they are identical. A negative control checks that the test can detect look-ahead.
    - **Baselines:** the historical rate per (oblast, hour of day) for the start model; the per-oblast median duration for the duration model.
    
    Results on the test period (14 Sep – 12 Oct 2026):
    
    | | Model | Baseline |
    |---|---|---|
    | Start: Brier ↓ | 0.150 | 0.166 |
    | Start: ROC-AUC ↑ | 0.771 | 0.724 |
    | Start: PR-AUC ↑ | 0.512 | 0.459 |
    | Duration: MAE of median ↓ | 19.3 min | 19.5 min |
    | Duration: 80% interval coverage | 81% | – |
    
    ## Layout
    
    ```
    training/   data loading, leakage-safe features, both models, train.py (W&B logging)
    app/        FastAPI server + static dashboard (d3); reuses training.features for live predictions
    models/     trained artifacts, metrics, replay data for the dashboard
    tests/      leakage tests
    exploration/ initial data profiling
    ```
    
    ## Known limitations (MVP)
    
    - "Now" mode: the daily dump lags up to ~24 h, so history-based features miss the latest alerts. Live alerts have no start time, so the duration curve is not conditioned on elapsed time.
    - A region under an active alert gets a low P(start), because a new alert can't start until the current one ends. This is correct for the label, but easy to misread on the map.
    - The duration point estimate is barely better than the per-oblast median. Its value is in the calibrated distribution.
[tool call: Bash]
    python3 - <<'EOF'
    p='CLAUDE.md'; s=open(p).read()
    a=s[s.index('## Layout & commands'):]
    s=s.replace(a,'''## Architecture
    
    - `training/` is the single source of truth for features. `features.py` holds the leakage-safe feature code; it works on alert intervals where `finished_at = NaT` means "still active". `data.truncate_at(alerts, t)` produces exactly that "knowable at t" view. `train.py` fits both models and writes everything to `models/`.
    - **Model 1** (`start_model.py`): LightGBM on an hourly (oblast × hour) grid. Label: an alert starts in `[t, t+1h)`.
    - **Model 2** (`duration_model.py`): lifelines log-normal AFT. Only its coefficients are exported to `models/duration_model.json`, and prediction (quantiles and survival curves conditioned on elapsed time) is closed-form numpy/scipy, checked against lifelines at train time.
    - `app/server.py` (FastAPI) imports `training.features` so live predictions use the same feature code. Replay mode reads `models/replay_*.parquet`. Now mode re-downloads the CSV (at most hourly) and overrides `active_now` / `n_active_oblasts` with the live ubilling feed.
    - `app/static/` is a no-build dashboard (d3 v7 from cdnjs). The map uses a planar `geoTransform` (x·cos 48.5°), because geoBoundaries polygon winding breaks d3's spherical projections.
    - Region names are mapped dataset ↔ geoBoundaries ↔ ubilling in `app/regions.py`. Luhansk is excluded from modelling (`data.EXCLUDED_REGIONS`); it has only pre-April-2022 rows.
    
    ## Commands
    
    The venv uses Python 3.13 (`requires-python >= 3.12`), managed by `uv`.
    - Train: `uv run python -m training.train [--refresh] [--no-wandb]` (~20 s). W&B reads `WANDB_API_KEY` from `.env` via python-dotenv. The file uses `KEY = value` with spaces, so don't `source` it.
    - Dashboard: `uv run python -m app [--port 8000]`
    - Tests: `uv run pytest`; single test: `uv run pytest tests/test_no_leakage.py::test_start_features_use_only_past`
    - Rebuild map file: `uv run python -m app.prepare_geojson`
    - `data/` (raw CSV) and `wandb/` are gitignored; `models/` is committed.
    ''')
    open(p,'w').write(s)
    EOF

==== CLAUDE (2026-10-12 12:35:23 UTC) ====
The MVP is built and trained. Both models train in **about 20 seconds total**, all 4 tests pass, and the dashboard works in both Replay and Now modes. The dashboard is still running at http://127.0.0.1:8765 if you want to look.

## Results on the last 28 days (14 Sep – 12 Oct)

| | Model | Baseline |
|---|---|---|
| Alert start: Brier score (lower is better) | **0.150** | 0.166 |
| Alert start: ROC‑AUC | **0.771** | 0.724 |
| Alert start: PR‑AUC | **0.512** | 0.459 |
| Duration: average error of the "most likely" time | 19.3 min | 19.5 min |
| Duration: real values inside the 80% range | 81% (target 80%) | – |

- **The start model is modestly but genuinely better than the baseline**, and its probabilities are well calibrated.
- **The duration model's single "most likely" time is no better than each oblast's usual median.** The useful part is the distribution, whose 80% range really does cover about 80% of alerts.
- Both W&B runs are in project `air-raid-alerts`. The second run shows the Luhansk fix.

## Errors caught along the way

- **I was wrong that Luhansk isn't in the data.** It has rows from early 2022, and training on its ~9,000 always-zero hours inflated ROC‑AUC from 0.771 to 0.783. I excluded it and retrained.
- **The leakage test is confirmed to catch a real leak.** It passed on the first try, so I deliberately injected a one-hour look-ahead into the feature code, and the test failed as it should.
- **Fixed: crash on regions with no alerts.** The feature code crashed when an oblast had no alerts in the time window. I fixed it and added a test.
- **Fixed: wrong data in Now mode.** Out-of-order responses could show the replay snapshot instead of live data, so only the latest request now gets drawn.
- **Fixed: map scale bug.** A d3 call silently broke the timeline cursor on the bottom chart.
- **Corrected in CLAUDE.md:** a note about Luhansk I'd written earlier that turned out to be wrong.

## Two problems for you to decide on

1. **Regions under an active alert show a low probability** (e.g. Zhytomyr at 4%), because a new alert can't start until the current one ends. That's correct for the label we chose, but misleading on a map. Options:
   - change the target to "is there an alert at any point in the next hour"
   - keep the target, but show active regions as "IN ALERT" with the time until it's expected to end, instead of a percentage
2. **Now mode mixes live and stale data.** The on-alert state is live, but the alert history comes from the once-a-day CSV, which today stops at 07:59 Kyiv. The fix would be to record live state changes while the app runs, which would also give us start times for live alerts. That means more work, and only Now mode benefits.

Nothing is committed yet. Should I commit and push to a GitHub repo? If so, give me the repo URL or tell me to create one; `gh` isn't installed here.

==== CLAUDE (2026-10-12 14:33:59 UTC) ====
I made your UI changes, plus one model change that follows from your decision. Answers to your questions first.

## 1. The models, and how they were trained

**Model 1: "Will an alert start in the next hour?"**
- **Algorithm:** LightGBM, a gradient-boosted decision tree model. It builds 400 small trees one after another, and each new tree corrects the errors of the earlier ones.
- **Loss:** log loss (binary cross-entropy). A confident wrong answer is punished heavily.
- **Inputs:**
  - oblast
  - hour of day and day of week
  - minutes since the last alert ended in this oblast
  - number of alerts in the last 24 h and 7 d
  - how many oblasts are on alert now
  - how many alerts started nationwide in the last hour
- **Data:** one row per oblast per hour. Training runs from 1 Oct 2025 to 14 Sep 2026; testing covers the last 28 days.
- **New:** following your decision, it's now trained and scored **only on hours when the oblast has no alert**. When there is an alert, Model 2 takes over.

**Model 2: "How long will this alert last?"**
- **Algorithm:** a log-normal survival model from the lifelines library. It assumes log(duration) follows a bell curve, with the center shifted by the inputs: oblast, hour, number of oblasts on alert, and the previous alert's length.
- **Loss:** negative log-likelihood with a small L2 penalty, minimized by the **BFGS** optimizer. I checked this in the library code.
- **Output:** a full probability curve, not just one number.

**How I optimized them: honestly, I didn't.** The settings (400 trees, learning rate 0.05, 31 leaves) are reasonable defaults I chose. There was no tuning and no validation set, and **no training curves exist**, because I only logged final numbers. That's also why **W&B looks confusing**: each run logs every metric once, so each chart is a single dot, not a line.

The proper fix is to:
- hold back the last ~2 weeks of the training period as a validation set
- log training and validation loss after every tree to W&B, so you get real curves
- use early stopping to choose the number of trees

That would make a good next step.

## 2. What the metrics mean

| Metric | Simple meaning | Ours vs baseline |
|---|---|---|
| **Brier score** | Average squared error of the probabilities (0 = perfect, lower is better) | 0.152 vs 0.168 |
| **ROC‑AUC** | If you take one hour where an alert started and one where it didn't, how often the model gives the first a higher number (0.5 = random) | 0.769 vs 0.730 |
| **PR‑AUC** | How well the model finds the hours with alert starts, without too many false alarms | 0.520 vs 0.479 |
| **Calibration** (chart) | When the model says 40%, does it really happen about 40% of the time? Dots on the diagonal mean yes | — |
| **Duration MAE** | Average error of the "most likely duration" in minutes | 19.3 vs 19.5 min |
| **80% coverage** | How often the real duration falls inside the model's 80% range. It should be about 80%, and it's 81% | — |
| **C‑index** | Whether the model ranks short and long alerts in the right order (0.5 = random) | 0.61 |

The **baseline** is the simplest honest guess. For Model 1 it's "how often an alert starts in this oblast at this hour of day historically". For Model 2 it's "the typical duration in this oblast".

## 3. Where the live data comes from

`https://ubilling.net.ua/aerialalerts/`. It's a free public service that says **only whether each oblast has an alert right now** (yes/no). Its own response says it gets this from an API by Vadym Klymenko, the same person behind our dataset. It's unofficial. Earlier today I checked it against the Air Force's Telegram posts and they matched.

## 4. Live vs stale data, simply

- Our **history file is updated once a day**. Today's copy ends at **07:59 Kyiv time**.
- The **live feed** is always current, but only says yes or no.

So at 17:30, the model knows which oblasts are on alert right now from the live feed. But when it asks "how many alerts were there in the last 24 hours?", it uses the history file, which **knows nothing about alerts after 07:59**. That part of the input is about 10 hours old: "stale".

The fix: while the app runs, it checks the live feed every minute and **records when alerts start and end**, building its own fresh history. That also gives us start times, so the predicted end time becomes more accurate.

## 5. UI changes made

- **Oblasts under alert are filled red**, with the most likely end time on top (e.g. `~17:42`). There's no probability for them anymore.
- **Oblast names are removed** from the map. Only plain white numbers remain, with no dark outline.
- **Ranking:** active alerts come first, soonest end first, then calm oblasts by risk.
- **Detail panel:** for an active oblast, the big number is now the expected end time.
- **Fixed:** the calibration chart showed a fake drop to 0% from a group with only **1 sample**. Groups with fewer than 30 are now hidden.

One caveat in Now mode: the live feed doesn't say when an alert started, so the end time assumes it **just started**. Alerts that have already been running a while will tend to end sooner than shown. The live recording above would fix this.

## 6. Ideas for the bottom row

1. **Today at a glance** (live): alerts today, total alert-hours, longest current alert, and the most-alerted oblast in the last 24 h. This is useful to a person, not just to someone grading the model.
2. **Did the model get it right?** A score for the recent period, for example "of the 5 highest-risk oblasts each hour, X% really got an alert", compared with the baseline. It's easier to understand than AUC.
3. **Performance over time:** daily score of model vs baseline across the test period. It shows whether the model gets worse as the situation changes.
4. **Where the model is weak:** a score per oblast, which shows where it struggles (e.g. quiet western oblasts).
5. **Duration check:** predicted vs actual duration for recent alerts, as a scatter plot or a "predicted end vs real end" list.
6. **Training curves:** after the W&B fix, a small train vs validation loss chart.

My suggestion is 1 + 2 + 3, with the model details (feature importance, calibration) moved to an "About the model" view.

The dashboard is running at http://127.0.0.1:8765. Nothing is committed yet. What should we do next: the training curves, the live recording, or the bottom row?

    /home/vumenira/projects/Air_raid_alert_analysis/training/build_neighbors.py
    """One-off: derive oblast adjacency from the map polygons -> training/neighbors.json.
    
    Adjacent oblasts share boundary vertices in the geoBoundaries file (distance 0), so the threshold is
    only a tolerance; the result is identical for 0.005-0.05 degrees.
    Usage: uv run python -m training.build_neighbors
    """
    import json
    from pathlib import Path
    
    import numpy as np
    
    from training.data import ROOT
    
    GEOJSON = ROOT / "app" / "static" / "oblasts.geojson"
    OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "neighbors.json"
    THRESHOLD_DEG = 0.01
    
    
    def vertices(geom: dict) -> np.ndarray:
        polys = geom["coordinates"] if geom["type"] == "MultiPolygon" else [geom["coordinates"]]
        return np.array([p for poly in polys for ring in poly for p in ring])
    
    
    def min_dist(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
        return min(np.sqrt(((a[i:i + 500, None] - b[None]) ** 2).sum(-1)).min() for i in range(0, len(a), 500))
    
    
    def main() -> None:
        feats = json.loads(GEOJSON.read_text())["features"]
        # permanent-alert areas are kept: e.g. Luhansk borders Kharkiv and Donetsk, but it never has alert starts
        V = {f["properties"]["region"]: vertices(f["geometry"]) for f in feats}
        names = sorted(V)
        nb = {n: [] for n in names}
        for i, x in enumerate(names):
            for y in names[i + 1:]:
                if min_dist(V[x], V[y]) < THRESHOLD_DEG:
                    nb[x].append(y)
                    nb[y].append(x)
        OUT.write_text(json.dumps(nb, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
        print(f"wrote {OUT}: {sum(map(len, nb.values())) // 2} adjacent pairs")
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()

[tool output]
    1	"""Leakage-safe features. Every feature at time ``t`` uses only alerts that started before ``t``
    2	and end times that were already in the past at ``t`` (an ongoing alert is known to be active,
    3	but not when it will end)."""
    4	import numpy as np
    5	import pandas as pd
    6	
    7	from training.data import KYIV_TZ
    8	
    9	HOUR = np.int64(3600 * 10**9)
    10	MAX_GAP_MIN = 7 * 24 * 60  # cap for "minutes since last alert ended"
    11	
    12	# active_now is computed but not a model input: the start model is only used when the oblast is calm.
    13	START_FEATURES = [
    14	    "region", "hour_kyiv", "dow_kyiv", "min_since_last_end",
    15	    "starts_24h", "starts_7d", "n_active_oblasts", "national_starts_1h",
    16	]
    17	DURATION_FEATURES = ["region", "hour_sin", "hour_cos", "n_active_oblasts", "prev_duration_min"]
    18	
    19	
    20	def _ns(ts) -> np.ndarray:
    21	    return pd.DatetimeIndex(ts).as_unit("ns").asi8
    22	
    23	
    24	def _intervals(alerts: pd.DataFrame, region: str) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    25	    a = alerts[alerts.region == region].sort_values("started_at")
    26	    starts = _ns(a.started_at)
    27	    ends = np.where(a.finished_at.isna(), np.iinfo(np.int64).max, _ns(a.finished_at.fillna(a.started_at)))
    28	    return starts, ends
    29	
    30	
    31	def _active_at(starts, ends, t) -> np.ndarray:
    32	    if len(starts) == 0:
    33	        return np.zeros(len(t), dtype=bool)
    34	    i = np.searchsorted(starts, t, side="left") - 1  # last alert that started strictly before t
    35	    return (i >= 0) & (ends[np.maximum(i, 0)] > t)
    36	
    37	
    38	def _count_between(starts, lo, hi) -> np.ndarray:
    39	    return np.searchsorted(starts, hi, side="left") - np.searchsorted(starts, lo, side="left")
    40	
    41	
    42	def hourly_grid(start: pd.Timestamp, end: pd.Timestamp) -> pd.DatetimeIndex:
    43	    return pd.date_range(start.ceil("h"), end.floor("h"), freq="h", tz="UTC")
    44	
    45	
    46	def start_features(alerts: pd.DataFrame, times: pd.DatetimeIndex, regions: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    47	    """One row per (region, t) with the features known at t."""
    48	    t = _ns(times)
    49	    per_region = {r: _intervals(alerts, r) for r in regions}
    50	    n_active = sum(_active_at(s, e, t).astype(int) for s, e in per_region.values())
    51	    all_starts = np.sort(_ns(alerts.started_at))
    52	    national_1h = _count_between(all_starts, t - HOUR, t)
    53	    local = times.tz_convert(KYIV_TZ)
    54	
    55	    frames = []
    56	    for r, (s, e) in per_region.items():
    57	        # ends of alerts that are already over at t (ongoing ones have end = int64 max)
    58	        finished_ends = np.sort(e[e != np.iinfo(np.int64).max])
    59	        j = np.searchsorted(finished_ends, t, side="right") - 1
    60	        last_end = finished_ends[np.maximum(j, 0)] if len(finished_ends) else np.zeros(len(t), dtype=np.int64)
    61	        gap = np.where(j >= 0, (t - last_end) / 6e10, MAX_GAP_MIN)
    62	        frames.append(pd.DataFrame({
    63	            "t": times,
    64	            "region": r,
    65	            "hour_kyiv": local.hour,
    66	            "dow_kyiv": local.dayofweek,
    67	            "active_now": _active_at(s, e, t).astype(int),
    68	            "min_since_last_end": np.minimum(gap, MAX_GAP_MIN),
    69	            "starts_24h": _count_between(s, t - 24 * HOUR, t),
    70	            "starts_7d": _count_between(s, t - 7 * 24 * HOUR, t),
    71	            "n_active_oblasts": n_active,
    72	            "national_starts_1h": national_1h,
    73	        }))
    74	    out = pd.concat(frames, ignore_index=True)
    75	    out["region"] = pd.Categorical(out.region, categories=regions)
    76	    return out
    77	
    78	
    79	def start_labels(alerts: pd.DataFrame, feats: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
    80	    """1 if an alert starts in [t, t + 1h) in that region."""
    81	    y = np.zeros(len(feats), dtype=int)
    82	    for r, idx in feats.groupby("region", observed=True).indices.items():
    83	        s, _ = _intervals(alerts, r)
    84	        t = _ns(feats.t.iloc[idx])
    85	        y[idx] = (_count_between(s, t, t + HOUR) > 0).astype(int)

    5	import argparse

    17	
    18	
    19	def split_times(alerts: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]:
    20	    # Last hour whose 1h label window is fully covered by the data snapshot.
    21	    grid_end = alerts.finished_at.max().floor("h") - pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
    22	    return grid_end - pd.Timedelta(days=TEST_DAYS), grid_end
    23	
    24	
    25	def init_wandb(enabled: bool, config: dict):
    26	    load_dotenv(ROOT / ".env")
    27	    if not enabled or not os.getenv("WANDB_API_KEY"):
    28	        print("W&B logging disabled")
    29	        return None
    30	    import wandb
    31	    return wandb.init(project="air-raid-alerts", config=config, job_type="train")
    32	
    33	
    34	def main() -> None:
    35	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    36	    ap.add_argument("--refresh", action="store_true", help="re-download the dataset")
    37	    ap.add_argument("--no-wandb", action="store_true")
    38	    args = ap.parse_args()
    39	
    40	    download(force=args.refresh)
    41	    alerts = load_alerts()
    42	    regs = regions(alerts)
    43	    test_start, grid_end = split_times(alerts)
    44	    print(f"data until {alerts.finished_at.max()} | train {TRAIN_START} -> {test_start} | test -> {grid_end}")
    45	    MODELS_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    46	
    47	    run = init_wandb(not args.no_wandb, {
    48	        "train_start": str(TRAIN_START), "test_start": str(test_start), "test_end": str(grid_end),
    49	        "test_days": TEST_DAYS, "n_regions": len(regs), "start_model": start_model.PARAMS,
    50	        "start_rounds": start_model.NUM_ROUNDS, "start_features": START_FEATURES,
    51	    })
    52	
    53	    # ---- Model 1: alert start within the next hour ----
    54	    t0 = time.time()
    55	    feats = start_features(alerts, hourly_grid(TRAIN_START, grid_end), regs)
    56	    y = start_labels(alerts, feats)
    57	    # Start model only applies to calm oblast-hours; during an alert the duration model takes over.
    58	    calm = (feats.active_now == 0).to_numpy()
    59	    train = (feats.t < test_start).to_numpy() & calm
    60	    test = (feats.t >= test_start).to_numpy() & calm
    61	    model = start_model.fit(feats[train], y[train])
    62	    p = start_model.predict(model, feats[test])
    63	    table = start_model.baseline_table(feats[train], y[train])
    64	    p_base = start_model.baseline_predict(table, feats[test])
    65	    m1 = {**start_model.metrics(y[test], p, "start"), **start_model.metrics(y[test], p_base, "start_baseline"),
    66	          "start_train_rows": int(train.sum()), "start_test_rows": int(test.sum()),
    67	          "start_test_pos_rate": float(y[test].mean()), "start_fit_seconds": time.time() - t0}
    68	    calib = start_model.calibration(y[test], p)
    69	    start_model.save(model, MODELS_DIR / "start_model.txt")
    70	
    71	    # ---- Model 2: alert duration distribution ----
    72	    t0 = time.time()
    73	    clean = alerts[~alerts.naive & alerts.finished_at.notna()]
    74	    tr_ev = clean[(clean.started_at >= TRAIN_START) & (clean.finished_at < test_start)]
    75	    te_ev = clean[(clean.started_at >= test_start) & (clean.started_at <= grid_end)]
    76	    params, aft = duration_model.fit(duration_features(alerts, tr_ev, regs), tr_ev.duration_min, regs)
    77	    te_feats = duration_features(alerts, te_ev, regs)
    78	    baseline_median = tr_ev.groupby("region").duration_min.median()
    79	    m2 = duration_model.evaluate(params, te_feats, te_ev.duration_min, baseline_median)
    80	    # sanity check: our closed-form median must match lifelines' own prediction
    81	    X_te = duration_model.design_matrix(te_feats, regs)
    82	    mu, sigma = duration_model.predict_mu_sigma(params, te_feats)
    83	    gap = np.max(np.abs(np.exp(mu) - aft.predict_median(X_te).to_numpy()))
    84	    assert gap < 1e-6 * np.exp(mu).max(), f"median mismatch vs lifelines: {gap}"
    85	    m2.update({"dur_train_events": len(tr_ev), "dur_test_events": len(te_ev), "dur_fit_seconds": time.time() - t0})
    86	    duration_model.save(params, MODELS_DIR / "duration_model.json")
    87	
    88	    # ---- Artifacts for the dashboard replay ----
    89	    # replay covers all test hours; p_start is NaN while the oblast is under alert
    90	    test_all = (feats.t >= test_start).to_numpy()
    91	    replay = feats[test_all][["t", "region", "active_now", "n_active_oblasts"]].assign(y=y[test_all])
    92	    replay["p_start"], replay["p_baseline"] = np.nan, np.nan
    93	    replay.loc[replay.active_now == 0, "p_start"] = p
    94	    replay.loc[replay.active_now == 0, "p_baseline"] = p_base
    95	    replay["region"] = replay.region.astype(str)
    96	    replay.to_parquet(MODELS_DIR / "replay_start.parquet", index=False)
    97	    lookback = test_start - pd.Timedelta(days=7)  # history panel needs a week before the test window
    98	    alerts[alerts.started_at >= lookback].to_parquet(MODELS_DIR / "replay_alerts.parquet", index=False)

    p='training/start_model.py'; s=open(p).read()
    a='''NUM_ROUNDS = 400
    
    
    def fit(X: pd.DataFrame, y: np.ndarray) -> lgb.Booster:
        return lgb.train(PARAMS, lgb.Dataset(X[START_FEATURES], y, categorical_feature=["region"]), NUM_ROUNDS)
    
    
    def predict(model: lgb.Booster, X: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
        return model.predict(X[START_FEATURES])
    '''
    b='''MAX_ROUNDS = 3000
    EARLY_STOPPING = 100  # stop when validation log loss hasn't improved for this many rounds
    
    
    def _dataset(X: pd.DataFrame, y: np.ndarray, features: list[str], reference=None) -> lgb.Dataset:
        cat = ["region"] if "region" in features else []
        return lgb.Dataset(X[features], y, categorical_feature=cat, reference=reference, free_raw_data=False)
    
    
    def fit_with_validation(X_tr, y_tr, X_va, y_va, features=START_FEATURES) -> tuple[lgb.Booster, dict]:
        """Early-stopped fit; returns the booster and per-round train/valid curves (log loss, AUC)."""
        curves: dict = {}
        dtr = _dataset(X_tr, y_tr, features)
        dva = _dataset(X_va, y_va, features, reference=dtr)
        booster = lgb.train(
            {**PARAMS, "metric": ["binary_logloss", "auc"]}, dtr, MAX_ROUNDS,
            valid_sets=[dtr, dva], valid_names=["train", "valid"],
            callbacks=[lgb.early_stopping(EARLY_STOPPING, first_metric_only=True, verbose=False),
                       lgb.record_evaluation(curves)],
        )
        return booster, curves
    
    
    def fit(X: pd.DataFrame, y: np.ndarray, rounds: int, features=START_FEATURES) -> lgb.Booster:
        """Final refit with a fixed number of rounds (chosen on validation)."""
        return lgb.train(PARAMS, _dataset(X, y, features), rounds)
    
    
    def predict(model: lgb.Booster, X: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
        # use the features the model was trained with; best_iteration is 0 (= all trees) for refit models
        return model.predict(X[model.feature_name()], num_iteration=model.best_iteration or None)
    '''
    assert a in s; s=s.replace(a,b); open(p,'w').write(s)

    def compare_families(f_tr: pd.DataFrame, d_tr: pd.Series, f_va: pd.DataFrame, d_va: pd.Series,
                         regions: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Mean validation log-likelihood (per alert, minutes scale) of each AFT distribution family."""
        tr = design_matrix(f_tr, regions).assign(duration=d_tr.clip(lower=0.5).to_numpy())
        va = design_matrix(f_va, regions).assign(duration=d_va.clip(lower=0.5).to_numpy())
        out = {}

            out[name] = float(m.score(va, scoring_method="log_likelihood"))
        return out

==== CLAUDE (2026-10-12 14:54:34 UTC) ====
Now the training script with the three-way split and the W&B curves:
[tool call: Write]
    /home/vumenira/projects/Air_raid_alert_analysis/training/train.py
    """Train both models on a time-ordered split and save artifacts.
    
        train [TRAIN_START, valid_start) -> valid [valid_start, test_start) -> test [test_start, end]
    
    Validation picks the number of boosting rounds (early stopping) and the duration distribution family;
    the final models are refit on train+valid and evaluated once on the test window.
    
    Usage: uv run python -m training.train [--refresh] [--no-wandb]
    """
    import argparse
    import json
    import os
    import time
    
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    from dotenv import load_dotenv
    
    from training import duration_model, start_model
    from training.data import MODELS_DIR, ROOT, TEST_DAYS, TRAIN_START, download, load_alerts, regions
    from training.features import START_FEATURES, duration_features, hourly_grid, start_features, start_labels
    
    VALID_DAYS = 14
    
    
    def split_times(alerts: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]:
        # Last hour whose 1h label window is fully covered by the data snapshot.
        grid_end = alerts.finished_at.max().floor("h") - pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
        test_start = grid_end - pd.Timedelta(days=TEST_DAYS)
        return test_start - pd.Timedelta(days=VALID_DAYS), test_start, grid_end
    
    
    def init_wandb(enabled: bool, config: dict):
        load_dotenv(ROOT / ".env")
        if not enabled or not os.getenv("WANDB_API_KEY"):
            print("W&B logging disabled")
            return None
        import wandb
        run = wandb.init(project="air-raid-alerts", config=config, job_type="train")
        run.define_metric("boosting_round")
        run.define_metric("start/*", step_metric="boosting_round")
        return run
    
    
    def start_feature_frame(alerts, regs, grid_end):
        feats = start_features(alerts, hourly_grid(TRAIN_START, grid_end), regs)
        return feats, start_labels(alerts, feats)
    
    
    def main() -> None:
        ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
        ap.add_argument("--refresh", action="store_true", help="re-download the dataset")
        ap.add_argument("--no-wandb", action="store_true")
        args = ap.parse_args()
    
        download(force=args.refresh)
        alerts = load_alerts()
        regs = regions(alerts)
        valid_start, test_start, grid_end = split_times(alerts)
        print(f"data until {alerts.finished_at.max()}\n  train {TRAIN_START} -> {valid_start}"
              f"\n  valid -> {test_start}\n  test  -> {grid_end}")
        MODELS_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
        run = init_wandb(not args.no_wandb, {
            "train_start": str(TRAIN_START), "valid_start": str(valid_start), "test_start": str(test_start),
            "test_end": str(grid_end), "valid_days": VALID_DAYS, "test_days": TEST_DAYS, "n_regions": len(regs),
            "start_params": start_model.PARAMS, "start_max_rounds": start_model.MAX_ROUNDS,
            "start_early_stopping": start_model.EARLY_STOPPING, "start_features": START_FEATURES,
        })
    
        # ---- Model 1: alert start within the next hour (calm oblast-hours only) ----
        t0 = time.time()
        feats, y = start_feature_frame(alerts, regs, grid_end)
        calm = (feats.active_now == 0).to_numpy()
        t = feats.t
        tr = ((t < valid_start).to_numpy()) & calm
        va = ((t >= valid_start) & (t < test_start)).to_numpy() & calm
        pre = ((t < test_start).to_numpy()) & calm
        te = ((t >= test_start).to_numpy()) & calm
    
        booster, curves = start_model.fit_with_validation(feats[tr], y[tr], feats[va], y[va])
        best_rounds = booster.best_iteration
        p_va = start_model.predict(booster, feats[va])
        model = start_model.fit(feats[pre], y[pre], best_rounds)  # refit on train+valid
        p = start_model.predict(model, feats[te])
        table = start_model.baseline_table(feats[pre], y[pre])
        p_base = start_model.baseline_predict(table, feats[te])
        m1 = {**start_model.metrics(y[te], p, "start"), **start_model.metrics(y[te], p_base, "start_baseline"),
              **start_model.metrics(y[va], p_va, "start_valid"),
              "start_best_rounds": best_rounds, "start_train_rows": int(pre.sum()), "start_test_rows": int(te.sum()),
              "start_test_pos_rate": float(y[te].mean()), "start_fit_seconds": time.time() - t0}
        calib = start_model.calibration(y[te], p)
        start_model.save(model, MODELS_DIR / "start_model.txt")
    
        # ---- Model 2: alert duration distribution ----
        t0 = time.time()
        clean = alerts[~alerts.naive & alerts.finished_at.notna()]
        ev_tr = clean[(clean.started_at >= TRAIN_START) & (clean.finished_at < valid_start)]
        ev_va = clean[(clean.started_at >= valid_start) & (clean.finished_at < test_start)]
        ev_pre = clean[(clean.started_at >= TRAIN_START) & (clean.finished_at < test_start)]
        ev_te = clean[(clean.started_at >= test_start) & (clean.started_at <= grid_end)]
        families = duration_model.compare_families(
            duration_features(alerts, ev_tr, regs), ev_tr.duration_min,
            duration_features(alerts, ev_va, regs), ev_va.duration_min, regs)
        best_family = max(families, key=families.get)
        if best_family != "lognormal":
            print(f"  NOTE: {best_family} beats lognormal on validation {families} - prediction code is lognormal-only")
        params, aft = duration_model.fit(duration_features(alerts, ev_pre, regs), ev_pre.duration_min, regs)
        te_feats = duration_features(alerts, ev_te, regs)
        baseline_median = ev_pre.groupby("region").duration_min.median()
        m2 = duration_model.evaluate(params, te_feats, ev_te.duration_min, baseline_median)
        # sanity check: our closed-form median must match lifelines' own prediction
        X_te = duration_model.design_matrix(te_feats, regs)
        mu, _ = duration_model.predict_mu_sigma(params, te_feats)
        gap = np.max(np.abs(np.exp(mu) - aft.predict_median(X_te).to_numpy()))
        assert gap < 1e-6 * np.exp(mu).max(), f"median mismatch vs lifelines: {gap}"
        m2.update({f"dur_valid_loglik_{k}": v for k, v in families.items()})
        m2.update({"dur_train_events": len(ev_pre), "dur_test_events": len(ev_te), "dur_fit_seconds": time.time() - t0})
        duration_model.save(params, MODELS_DIR / "duration_model.json")
    
        # ---- Artifacts for the dashboard replay ----
        # replay covers all test hours; p_start is NaN while the oblast is under alert
        test_all = (t >= test_start).to_numpy()
        replay = feats[test_all][["t", "region", "active_now", "n_active_oblasts"]].assign(y=y[test_all])
        replay["p_start"], replay["p_baseline"] = np.nan, np.nan
        replay.loc[replay.active_now == 0, "p_start"] = p
        replay.loc[replay.active_now == 0, "p_baseline"] = p_base
        replay["region"] = replay.region.astype(str)
        replay.to_parquet(MODELS_DIR / "replay_start.parquet", index=False)
        lookback = test_start - pd.Timedelta(days=7)  # history panel needs a week before the test window
        alerts[alerts.started_at >= lookback].to_parquet(MODELS_DIR / "replay_alerts.parquet", index=False)
        importance = dict(zip(model.feature_name(), model.feature_importance("gain").round(1).tolist()))
        curve_rows = [{"round": i + 1, **{f"{split}_{m}": curves[split][m][i] for split in curves for m in curves[split]}}
                      for i in range(len(curves["train"]["binary_logloss"]))]
    
        metrics = {**m1, **m2, "valid_start": str(valid_start), "test_start": str(test_start),
                   "test_end": str(grid_end), "data_until": str(alerts.finished_at.max())}
        (MODELS_DIR / "metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps(
            {"metrics": metrics, "calibration": calib.to_dict("records"), "importance": importance,
             "curves": curve_rows}, indent=1))
    
        for k, v in metrics.items():
            print(f"  {k:30s} {v:.4f}" if isinstance(v, float) else f"  {k:30s} {v}")
    
        if run:
            import wandb
            for row in curve_rows:  # one step per boosting round -> real training curves
                run.log({"boosting_round": row["round"],
                         "start/train_logloss": row["train_binary_logloss"], "start/valid_logloss": row["valid_binary_logloss"],
                         "start/train_auc": row["train_auc"], "start/valid_auc": row["valid_auc"]})
            run.summary.update({k: v for k, v in metrics.items() if isinstance(v, (int, float))})
            run.log({
                "start_calibration": wandb.plot.line(wandb.Table(dataframe=calib), "mean_pred", "frac_pos",
                                                     title="Calibration (test)"),
                "start_importance": wandb.plot.bar(wandb.Table(data=list(importance.items()), columns=["feature", "gain"]),
                                                   "feature", "gain", title="Feature importance (gain)"),
                "duration_families": wandb.plot.bar(wandb.Table(data=list(families.items()), columns=["family", "valid_loglik"]),
                                                    "family", "valid_loglik", title="Duration family: validation log-likelihood"),
            })
            run.finish()
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()
[tool output]

    """Feature ablation for the start model, scored on the VALIDATION window only (test stays untouched).
    
    Usage: uv run python exploration/02_feature_ablation.py [--wandb]
    """
    import argparse
    
    from training import start_model
    from training.data import load_alerts, regions
    from training.features import START_FEATURES
    from training.train import init_wandb, split_times, start_feature_frame
    
    BASE = [f for f in START_FEATURES if f not in ("nb_active_frac", "nb_starts_1h")]
    SINCOS = lambda fs: [f for f in fs if f not in ("hour_kyiv", "dow_kyiv")] + ["hour_sin", "hour_cos", "dow_sin", "dow_cos"]
    VARIANTS = {
        "raw_time": BASE,
        "sincos_time": SINCOS(BASE),
        "raw_time+neighbors": BASE + ["nb_active_frac", "nb_starts_1h"],
        "sincos_time+neighbors": SINCOS(BASE) + ["nb_active_frac", "nb_starts_1h"],
    }
    
    
    def main() -> None:
        ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
        ap.add_argument("--wandb", action="store_true")
        args = ap.parse_args()
        alerts = load_alerts()
        regs = regions(alerts)
        valid_start, test_start, grid_end = split_times(alerts)
        feats, y = start_feature_frame(alerts, regs, grid_end)
        calm = (feats.active_now == 0).to_numpy()
        tr = (feats.t < valid_start).to_numpy() & calm
        va = ((feats.t >= valid_start) & (feats.t < test_start)).to_numpy() & calm
    
        print(f"{'variant':24s} {'rounds':>6s} {'logloss':>8s} {'brier':>7s} {'auc':>6s} {'pr_auc':>6s}")
        for name, fs in VARIANTS.items():
            booster, curves = start_model.fit_with_validation(feats[tr], y[tr], feats[va], y[va], features=fs)
            m = start_model.metrics(y[va], start_model.predict(booster, feats[va]), "valid")
            print(f"{name:24s} {booster.best_iteration:6d} {m['valid_logloss']:8.4f} {m['valid_brier']:7.4f} "
                  f"{m['valid_roc_auc']:6.4f} {m['valid_pr_auc']:6.4f}")
            if args.wandb:
                run = init_wandb(True, {"variant": name, "features": fs})
                run.tags = ("ablation",)
                for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(curves["train"]["binary_logloss"], curves["valid"]["binary_logloss"])):
                    run.log({"boosting_round": i + 1, "start/train_logloss": a, "start/valid_logloss": b})
                run.summary.update({**m, "best_rounds": booster.best_iteration})
                run.finish()
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()

    --- old
    _live_cache: dict = {}
    +++ new
    DURATION_BINS = [0, 10, 20, 30, 60, 120, np.inf]
    DURATION_LABELS = ["<10m", "10–20m", "20–30m", "30–60m", "1–2h", "2h+"]
    
    
    @app.get("/api/glance")
    def glance(t: str, period: str = "today", live: bool = False):
        """Situation summary for 'today' (Kyiv calendar day up to t) or the last 7 days, using only alerts known at t."""
        a = artifacts()
        end = pd.Timestamp(t)
        start = (end.tz_convert("Europe/Kyiv").normalize().tz_convert("UTC") if period == "today"
                 else end - pd.Timedelta(days=7))
        al = load_alerts() if live else a["alerts"]
        al = al[al.region.isin(a["regions"]) & (al.started_at < end)]
        fin = al.finished_at.where(al.finished_at.notna() & (al.finished_at <= end), end)  # ongoing -> clipped at t
        w = al[fin > start].assign(s=lambda d: d.started_at.clip(lower=start), e=fin[fin > start])
        hours = ((w.e - w.s).dt.total_seconds() / 3600)
        started = w[w.started_at >= start]
        done = started[started.finished_at.notna() & (started.finished_at <= end) & ~started.naive.astype(bool)]
    
        # how many oblasts were under alert over time: count(start <= s) - count(end <= s)
        step = "10min" if period == "today" else "1h"
        grid = pd.date_range(start.ceil(step), end, freq=step)
        s_sorted, e_sorted = np.sort(w.s.values), np.sort(w.e.values)
        active = np.searchsorted(s_sorted, grid.values, "right") - np.searchsorted(e_sorted, grid.values, "right")
    
        by_region = (w.assign(h=hours).groupby("region").agg(hours=("h", "sum"))
                     .join(started.groupby("region").size().rename("alerts")).fillna(0)
                     .sort_values("hours", ascending=False))
        longest = (w.assign(m=(w.e - w.started_at).dt.total_seconds() / 60).sort_values("m").iloc[-1]
                   if len(w) else None)
        peak_i = int(np.argmax(active)) if len(active) else None
        dur_hist = pd.cut(done.duration_min, DURATION_BINS, labels=DURATION_LABELS).value_counts().reindex(DURATION_LABELS)
        return {
            "period": period, "start": start.isoformat(), "end": end.isoformat(),
            "data_until": al.finished_at.max().isoformat() if live and len(al) else None,
            "alerts_started": int(len(started)),
            "alert_hours": round(float(hours.sum()), 1),
            "oblasts_affected": int(w.region.nunique()), "oblasts_total": len(a["regions"]),
            "median_duration_min": opt(done.duration_min.median()),
            "longest": None if longest is None else {"region": longest.region, "minutes": float(longest.m),
                                                     "ongoing": bool(pd.isna(longest.finished_at) or longest.finished_at > end)},
            "peak": None if peak_i is None else {"oblasts": int(active[peak_i]), "t": grid[peak_i].isoformat()},
            "by_region": [{"region": r, "label": LABELS[r], "hours": round(float(x.hours), 2), "alerts": int(x.alerts)}
                          for r, x in by_region.iterrows()],
            "timeline": {"t": [x.isoformat() for x in grid], "active": active.tolist()},
            "durations": [{"bin": k, "n": int(v)} for k, v in dur_hist.items()],
        }

    248	function renderKpis() {
    249	  const m = state.meta.metrics;
    250	  const tile = (k, v, d, cls = "") => `<div class="kpi"><span class="k">${k}</span><span class="v">${v}</span><span class="d ${cls}">${d}</span></div>`;
    251	  const rel = (a, b, lowerBetter) => {
    252	    const diff = ((a - b) / b) * 100, better = lowerBetter ? diff < 0 : diff > 0;
    253	    return [`${diff > 0 ? "+" : ""}${diff.toFixed(1)}% vs baseline ${b.toFixed(3)}`, better ? "better" : "worse"];
    254	  };
    255	  const [bd, bc] = rel(m.start_brier, m.start_baseline_brier, true);
    256	  const [ad, ac] = rel(m.start_roc_auc, m.start_baseline_roc_auc, false);
    257	  const [pd_, pc] = rel(m.start_pr_auc, m.start_baseline_pr_auc, false);
    258	  const [md, mc] = rel(m.dur_mae_median_min, m.dur_mae_baseline_min, true);
    259	  $("kpis").innerHTML =
    260	    tile("Start · Brier", m.start_brier.toFixed(3), bd, bc) +
    261	    tile("Start · ROC-AUC", m.start_roc_auc.toFixed(3), ad, ac) +
    262	    tile("Start · PR-AUC", m.start_pr_auc.toFixed(3), pd_, pc) +
    263	    tile("Duration · MAE", `${m.dur_mae_median_min.toFixed(1)}m`, md.replace(/baseline ([\d.]+)/, "baseline $1m"), mc) +
    264	    tile("Duration · 80% cover", pct(m.dur_coverage_80), "target 80%", Math.abs(m.dur_coverage_80 - 0.8) < 0.05 ? "better" : "worse") +
    265	    tile("Duration · C-index", m.dur_c_index.toFixed(3), `${m.dur_test_events} test alerts`);
    266	}

    296	  nationalCursor = f.g.append("line").attr("class", "cursor").attr("y1", 0).attr("y2", f.ih);
    297	  nationalX = x; // note: selection.datum(fn) would *call* fn, so keep the scale in a variable
    298	  hoverTime(f, x, ts, (i) => `${fmtKyiv.format(ts[i])}<br><b>${n.active[i]}</b> oblasts under alert<br><span class="mono">${n.starts[i]} alerts started in the next hour · model expected ${n.p_sum[i].toFixed(1)}</span><br><span class="mono">click to jump</span>`,
    299	    (i) => { if (state.mode === "replay") setIdx(i); });
    300	}
    301	
    302	function drawImportance() {
    303	  const names = { region: "Oblast", hour_kyiv: "Hour of day", dow_kyiv: "Day of week", active_now: "Alert active now", min_since_last_end: "Time since last alert",
    304	    starts_24h: "Alerts last 24 h", starts_7d: "Alerts last 7 d", n_active_oblasts: "Oblasts on alert now", national_starts_1h: "National starts 1 h" };
    305	  const data = Object.entries(state.meta.importance).map(([k, v]) => ({ k: names[k] ?? k, v })).sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v);
    306	  const total = d3.sum(data, (d) => d.v);
    307	  const f = frame($("importance"), { t: 6, r: 40, b: 6, l: 150 });
    308	  const y = d3.scaleBand(data.map((d) => d.k), [0, f.ih]).padding(0.3), x = d3.scaleLinear([0, d3.max(data, (d) => d.v)], [0, f.iw]);
    309	  f.g.append("g").attr("class", "axis").call(d3.axisLeft(y).tickSize(0).tickPadding(8)).select(".domain").remove();
    310	  f.g.selectAll("rect").data(data).join("rect").attr("y", (d) => y(d.k)).attr("height", y.bandwidth()).attr("width", (d) => x(d.v))
    311	    .attr("rx", 2).attr("fill", css("--series-model"))
    312	    .on("mousemove", (ev, d) => showTip(ev, `<b>${d.k}</b><br>${pct(d.v / total)} of total gain`)).on("mouseleave", hideTip);
    313	  f.g.selectAll("text.v").data(data).join("text").attr("class", "series-label").attr("x", (d) => x(d.v) + 4)
    314	    .attr("y", (d) => y(d.k) + y.bandwidth() / 2 + 3).text((d) => pct(d.v / total));
    315	}
    316	
    317	/* ---------- orchestration ---------- */
    318	function render() {
    319	  const t = new Date(state.snap.t);
    320	  $("clock-kyiv").textContent = `${fmtKyiv.format(t)} Kyiv`;
    321	  $("clock-sub").textContent = state.mode === "replay"
    322	    ? `forecast issued at ${t.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace("T", " ")} UTC`
    323	    : `live · refreshes every 60 s`;
    324	  $("status-line").innerHTML = state.mode === "replay"
    325	    ? `test period ${fmtShort.format(new Date(state.meta.times[0]))} – ${fmtShort.format(new Date(state.meta.times.at(-1)))}`
    326	    : `live state: ${state.snap.live_source ? "ubilling feed" : '<span class="warn">unavailable</span>'} · history until ${fmtKyiv.format(new Date(state.snap.history_until))}`;
    327	  if (!state.selected) state.selected = [...state.snap.regions].sort((a, b) => (b.p_start ?? -1) - (a.p_start ?? -1))[0].region;
    328	  renderMap();
    329	  renderRanking();
    330	  renderDetail();
    331	  if (nationalCursor) {
    332	    nationalCursor.style("display", state.mode === "replay" ? null : "none").attr("x1", nationalX(t)).attr("x2", nationalX(t));
    333	  }
    334	}
    335	
    336	function select(region) {
    337	  state.selected = region;
    338	  renderMap(); renderRanking(); renderDetail();
    339	}
    340	
    341	function setIdx(i) {
    342	  state.idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.meta.times.length - 1, i));
    343	  $("slider").value = state.idx;
    344	  loadSnapshot();
    345	}
    346	
    347	function setMode(mode) {
    348	  state.mode = mode;
    349	  document.body.classList.toggle("now", mode === "now");
    350	  $("mode-replay").classList.toggle("active", mode === "replay");
    351	  $("mode-now").classList.toggle("active", mode === "now");
    352	  $("timeline").classList.toggle("disabled", mode === "now");
    353	  stopPlay();
    354	  clearInterval(state.nowTimer);
    355	  if (mode === "now") state.nowTimer = setInterval(loadSnapshot, 60_000);
    356	  loadSnapshot();
    357	}
    358	
    359	function stopPlay() { clearInterval(state.timer); state.timer = null; $("play").textContent = "▶"; }
    360	function togglePlay() {
    361	  if (state.timer) return stopPlay();
    362	  $("play").textContent = "❚❚";
    363	  state.timer = setInterval(() => (state.idx >= state.meta.times.length - 1 ? stopPlay() : setIdx(state.idx + 1)), 700);
    364	}
    365	
    366	async function init() {
    367	  [state.meta, state.geo] = await Promise.all([getJSON("/api/meta"), getJSON("/static/oblasts.geojson")]);
    368	  $("ramp").style.background = `linear-gradient(90deg, ${RAMP.map((c, i) => `${c} ${(i / RAMP.length) * 100}% ${((i + 1) / RAMP.length) * 100}%`).join(",")})`;
    369	  const slider = $("slider");
    370	  slider.max = state.meta.times.length - 1;
    371	  state.idx = state.meta.times.length - 1;
    372	  slider.value = state.idx;
    373	  let deb;
    374	  slider.addEventListener("input", () => { stopPlay(); clearTimeout(deb); state.idx = +slider.value; deb = setTimeout(loadSnapshot, 80); });
    375	  $("play").addEventListener("click", togglePlay);
    376	  $("mode-replay").addEventListener("click", () => setMode("replay"));
    377	  $("mode-now").addEventListener("click", () => setMode("now"));
    378	  drawMap(); renderKpis(); drawCalibration(); drawNational(); drawImportance();
    379	  window.addEventListener("resize", () => { drawMap(); drawCalibration(); drawNational(); drawImportance(); if (state.snap) render(); });
    380	  await loadSnapshot();
    381	}
    382	
    383	init().catch((e) => { document.body.insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", `<pre class="warn" style="padding:16px">${e.message}</pre>`); });

I'm replacing the old bottom-row code (lines 247–315) with a three-tab version. The calibration and importance charts are kept, but each now draws into whichever card it's given:
